# Financial Statement Review control parity

Status: **baseline incomplete**. On 26 November 2026 the user chose a rolling
redesign of all FSR tabs and the shared shell: inventory each area before
changing it. This inventory does not establish full parity.

Branch: `feat/fsr-ui-redesign`, from `main` at `e4d2502`, with pre-existing
staged and unstaged work retained.
Continue to enumerate conditional controls, grid columns, menus, shortcuts,
dialogs, handlers and states before changing the corresponding unverified areas.
Include AG Grid generated controls, branch/consolidated variants and screens
absent from the mockups. Use synthetic data.

and 2560x1440 using an isolated synthetic profile. Bounds and two-column
layout passed automated checks; the 1280x720 and 1536x864 screenshots were
visually inspected. Initial focus, forward/reverse focus trapping, Escape,
Cancel and Close passed in the same isolated harness. These are not a
full-module responsive, accessibility or parity test.

The Master and FSR-header screenshots at 1280x720 and 1536x864 in
`%TEMP%\icai-vera-fsg-ui-TkdlPn\screenshots` were visually inspected.
The requested approximately 152px maximum chrome height has not been achieved;
navigation geometry was restored after a reduced-height version failed three
submenu checks, and its interaction needs further investigation.

## Synthetic runtime observations (partial)

The existing UI harness was instrumented in memory at its screenshot points;
the tracked harness was not edited. Its isolated run passed 139/139 checks.
The JSON snapshots in `%TEMP%\icai-vera-fsg-ui-SafPvD\screenshots` record
rendered elements inside `.fsg-root`, their available labels and selected
state attributes, alongside screenshots. These temporary files may not persist.

The original snapshot's unlabeled elements in Review Financials, Reco, Notes,
Annex and the two investment views are checkbox inputs, not necessarily
unlabeled to a user: the capture did not read associated `<label>` text.
Source identifies the five Review Financials checkboxes as Balance Sheet and
Profit & Loss (both checked and disabled), Notes, Annexures and the dedicated
signing page; the last three retain separate handlers. Source also identifies
the Reco / Annex `showZero` checkbox and the Annex-only `showEmptyAnnex`
checkbox. A second capture with associated label text confirmed those labels,
the Notes toolbar's Empty notes / Zero-balance rows, and the conditional
Include Dep (Comp) Fixed Assets Schedule in this note checkbox. It passed
139/139 checks; its temporary snapshots are under
`%TEMP%\icai-vera-fsg-ui-BjPWzR\screenshots`.

These are element counts, not counts of distinct verified controls. The original
snapshot selection omitted native expanders; both captures omit the surrounding
VERA shell, unopened menus and dialogs, and hidden states. Neither capture
establishes viewport visibility; labels may be blank, and neither records
handlers or actual filter semantics. The harness independently
exercised selected behavior, but its passing checks do not prove complete
control parity. Match observations to source and exercise the missing
variants before declaring this inventory complete.

found additional Current Year / Previous Year headings after the sweep had
visited other screens. The cause was not established. Earlier unswept runs
passed 139/139; neither result verifies every control.

must remain available wherever currently enabled; verify the generated
controls in the running app as well as the JSX candidates below.

Selection, code and name are pinned left; Status and Actions are pinned right. The pinned TOTAL is also passed to the row-action renderer; do not replace its handlers with the separate selected-row bulk handlers. |

These are source observations, not a complete control inventory or approval
to change semantics. Actual TB and Mapped TB show different data and amount
filters; do not homogenize them by changing behavior.

For the numeric columns of Mapped TB and Mapped TB with Remarks,
`agAmountFilter` supplies an AG Grid text floating filter. It accepts signed
comparisons (`>`, `<`, `>=`, `<=`, `=`), plain equality and ranges; blank or
unreadable text passes all rows. Mapped TB compares the displayed unit
(including Lakhs / Crores); Remarks uses its default divisor. The floating
filter button is suppressed for these amount columns. Actual TB instead
assigns AG Grid's number filter to its five numeric fields. Rectification
uses the same amount parser in its own table, not an AG Grid. Keep these
differences and the existing 250 ms filter debounce.

The installed `ag-grid-community` and `ag-grid-react` versions are 31.3.4.
Their default text-filter operators are Contains, Does not contain, Equals,
Does not equal, Begins with, Ends with, Blank and Not blank; default number-filter
operators are Equals, Not equal, Greater than, Greater than or equal, Less
than, Less than or equal, In range, Blank and Not blank. The isolated runtime
capture opened the text-filter popup and operator list on Actual TB, Mapped TB
and Mapped TB with Remarks. Each displayed these eight operators and a Filter
Value input; Mapping Rules displayed the same choices. A separate synthetic
capture opened Actual TB's Opening Bal. number filter: Equals, Does not equal,
Greater than, Greater than or equal to, Less than, Less than or equal to,
Between, Blank and Not blank. The same run observed no popup button on the
Mapped TB and Remarks custom amount floating filters. These defaults must not
be applied to the custom amount-filter columns. Other columns and combined
filter conditions still need individual runtime verification.

FSR shortcut handling ignores focused `input`, `textarea` and `select` for
top-tab navigation and categorisation undo/redo; menu letter handling also
ignores content-editable areas. Preserve these distinctions rather than
assuming every shortcut applies in every focus state.

| Tally Excel / Real Books / Zoho Books / Busy upload | Close / backdrop / Escape; Cancel; Upload | Separate dialog's `onCancel` and `handleUpload`; Cancel disabled while busy, Upload disabled when `!canUpload || busy`. Preserve each format's inputs and import route. | Corresponding format dialog |
| Tally Auto-Import | Tally company; Financial Year; From Date; To Date; Branch | Company select only when multiple companies are available; `setSelectedCompany`, `pickYear`, date setters and `setBranchName`. Controls appear when connection status is ready; year and dates are required, branch applies to branch entities. | Tally Auto-Import dialog |
| Tally Auto-Import | Close / backdrop / Escape; Cancel; Import from Tally | `onCancel`, `handleImport`; Import appears in ready state and checks missing inputs, while an importing-state button is disabled. Preserve external Tally behavior. | Tally Auto-Import dialog |
| Financial Period picker | Financial Period / Financial Year and each period; custom period / return to list | Calls `onChange` with selected period ID, dates, year and text; locked periods are disabled. Custom text and return button appear only where `allowCustom` permits them. | Existing period picker in each form |
| Branch picker | Branch select; Add new branch; New branch name; Add branch; Cancel | `onChange`, `setAdding`, `setDraft`, `add`; Enter also adds. With no existing branches it starts in add mode and Cancel is absent. Existing duplicate-name handling and server errors stay. | Existing branch picker in upload dialogs |
| New TB version carry-over | Copy from prior version; Yes / No for manually entered details; one checkbox per available area | `setFromId`, `setCopyDetails`, `toggle`; Copy from appears only for multiple prior versions; details radios only when areas exist and checkboxes only when Yes. Default-on areas and separately unticked adjustments / journal entries remain. | Version carry dialog |
| New TB version carry-over | Yes / No for Learned Ledger Rules; Continue | `setCarryRules` and `confirm` pass separate selections to `onConfirm`; Continue disabled while areas load or while busy. `useModalKeys` has a no-op close callback; do not add a dismiss path that changes this behavior. | Version carry dialog |
| Workings carry / upload | One checkbox per available area; Skip / close / backdrop; confirm | `toggle`, `onClose`, `confirm`; controls are disabled or close is suppressed while busy; confirm disabled with no selection. Preserve this dialog separately from the version-carry prompt. | Workings dialog |

`useModalKeys` supplies Escape, Tab trapping, initial focus and, when an
`onConfirm` callback is supplied, Ctrl/Cmd+Enter. Its presence is per-dialog,
not universal; inspect each dialog rather than assuming it restores focus or
supports confirmation.

## Previous-year and rectification controls

| Old location | Control | Handler / current condition | Proposed location |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Actual TB > Add Manual Row | Ledger Name, Ledger Code, Opening Balance, Debit, Credit, Closing Balance | Each `onChange` updates its corresponding `addRowData` field; name is required and focused initially. Values are passed to `insertManualRow` without changing the existing parsing or signs. | Add Manual Row dialog |
| Actual TB > Add Manual Row | Close icon, backdrop, Cancel; Add Row | Close / Cancel set `showAddRow` false; `handleInsertRow` saves and reloads, closes and clears fields on success. Add Row is disabled when name is blank. | Add Manual Row dialog header / footer |
| Actual TB > Delete row | Delete this row; Cancel / Delete | Row button stores that specific row in `rowToDelete`; confirmation `handleDeleteRow` deletes that row, reloads and closes on success; preserve deleting state and errors. | Row action / confirmation |
| Tools dialog | Download All Tables (Excel); Download Format Reference (HTML); Close, backdrop, Escape | `handleExportAllTables` and `handleDownloadFormatReference` are distinct actions with independent busy / result state; `onClose` handles closing. All Tables covers FSR tables across clients, is restricted to partners / admins in the main process, and is not a full VERA backup. | Tools dialog |
| Mapped TB > Bulk Override | Apply same mapping to all; Override one by one; Cancel / close | `setMode('same')` or `setMode('onebyone')` opens `OverrideModal` with different save / close callbacks; Cancel and close call `onClose`. | Bulk Override dialog |
| Mapped TB > Bulk Override, same | Override form Save / close | Save calls `overrideMany` on selected row IDs and `onDone`; close calls `onClose`. | Bulk override form |
| Mapped TB > Bulk Override, one by one | Override form Save / Skip; Done | Save calls `overrideRow` then advances; close is labelled Skip and advances or calls `onDone`; Done appears after all rows and calls `onDone`. | Per-ledger override form / completion |
| Mapped TB / Annex > Override Mapping | Mapping 1, 2, 3 and LevelFormat Row | Cascading selectors `setM1`, `setM2`, `setM3`, `setLfId`; changing earlier mappings clears downstream values as currently implemented. Format rows and slash-separated alternatives depend on the selected entity / rule set. | Override form |
| Mapped TB / Annex > Override Mapping | Mapping 4, 5, 6, 7 (Nature); Override Note (reason) | Setters `setL4` through `setL7` and `setNote`; mapping levels render a select for slash alternatives, otherwise a text input. Existing inferred format and default selection logic stays unchanged. | Override form |
| Mapped TB > Add Ledger | Ledger name, Ledger code, Adjustments (Debit), Adjustments (Credit) | `setLedgerName`, `setLedgerCode`, `setAdjD`, `setAdjC`; same mapping and reason fields as Override Mapping. Name and Mapping 1 are required by `handleSave`; adjustment inputs have `min=0` and `step=0.01`. | Add Ledger form |
| Override Mapping / Add Ledger | Close, backdrop, Cancel / Skip; Save Override / Add Ledger; Escape / Ctrl+Enter | `onClose` and `handleSave`; the parent may give close a distinct Skip behavior. Save disabled while saving; existing validation and error display differ by parent. | Form header / footer |
| Mapped TB > Resolve Mapping Conflict | Candidate buttons; Cancel / close / backdrop; Confirm Selection | Each candidate calls `setSelected`; Enter / Space also select. `handleResolve` requires selection, calls `onResolve(row.id, selected)`, and is disabled without selection or while saving; Escape / Ctrl+Enter use `useModalKeys`. | Conflict dialog |
| Mapped TB > Mapping Discrepancy | Suggested mapping radios; Keep Tally Group; Accept Suggestion; Close / backdrop | Radio `setSelected(i)` defaults to first option; `handleKeep` and `handleAccept` invoke different `onResolve` decisions; both disabled while saving. Escape closes and Ctrl+Enter accepts. | Discrepancy dialog |
| Statement and export toolbars | Previous Year figures: Required / Not required, column shows dash / Not required, column left out | `PYModeSelect.change` calls `setMode`, then `onChanged`; present only with entity period ID | Shared previous-year control; retain all three distinct values |
| Applicable FSR toolbars | Previous Year TB / None (use opening balances) / each available TB | `PYTBSelector` excludes current TB and returns null if no alternatives; calls supplied `onChange`, respecting supplied `disabled` / title | Shared previous-year control, not the mode selector |
| BS / PL / Notes | Manual PY toggle | `ManualPYButton.toggle` calls `manualPY.setEnabled` and `onChanged`; branch/PY selection can disable it | Previous-year group, preserving gating |
| Rectification toolbar and scoped banner | Current Year / Previous Year | `setCategorisePY`; PY disabled without selected previous-year TB; both toolbar and scoped banner variants exist | Rectification toolbar / scope |
| Rectification toolbar | Previous Year TB; Run Auto-Mapping (PY); Show all mapped rows; Include zero-balance ledgers; Refresh; Search; Clear filters | `onPrevTBChange`, `handleRunAutoMap`, `setShowAll`, `setShowZero`, `refresh`, `setSearch`, `clearFilters`; auto-map appears only in PY | Rectification toolbar |
| Rectification scope | Clear scope; Look in other year; Open previous-year ledgers | `setScopeFilter(null)` or `setCategorisePY`; conditional on scope, empty results and previous TB | Scope / empty-state actions |
| Rectification table | Selection; Ledger in Books; CY/PY; Dr/Cr; Save; To fix; Mapping 1-3; Status; Books ALIE; Books Parent; Group and Subgroups 1-3 | Header `toggleAll` selects all filtered visible rows across pages; row `toggleRow`, ledger focus `setFocusedRow`, row `saveOne`, mapping selects. Preserve per-column filter controls. | Rectification table |
| Rectification filter row | Amount; Dr / Cr; To fix; text fields | `setFilter` with respective column key. Amount uses `parseAmountFilter` and indicates invalid syntax; Dr / Cr has All / Dr / Cr; To fix has All / To fix (anything) / Complete / each discovered need; text columns use independent Filter inputs. | Table filter row |
| Rectification mapping row | Mapping 1 / 2 / 3; Custom line or Custom note line | `updateLedgerLocal` resets later mapping levels when an earlier level changes; Mapping 2/3 are disabled until their parent is selected; existing out-of-format value remains visible. Custom choices open the shared custom editor. | Per-row mapping cells |
| Rectification table | Mapping 4 (sub-head), 5 (nature), 6 (Secured / Unsecured), 7 (specify nature) | Conditional nature columns; row editor and custom note line retain existing behavior | Rectification table |
| Rectification table | Previous / Next; Rows per page 100 / 200 / 500 | `setPage`, `setPageSize`; pager shown when visible count exceeds 100 | Table pagination |
| Rectification | Bulk apply selections and Save All Changes | Existing bulk callbacks and `dirtyIds`; retain separate row Save and selected-row behavior | Bulk toolbar |
| Rectification bulk toolbar | Mapping 1 / 2 / 3; Custom line or Custom note line; Mapping 4-7 nature fields; Apply to selected | `setBulkM1`, `setBulkM2`, `setBulkM3` reset downstream values; `openCustomEditor` accepts custom captions, and `setBulkNature` / `setBulkL5` control conditional free-text or select fields. `applyBulk` requires at least one selected row and Mapping 1, and is disabled while saving. | Bulk toolbar |
| Rectification bulk toolbar | Mapping 4-7 only: per-level value and Set on N | `setLevelOnly`, `applyLevelOnly(p)` apply the selected level only to eligible selected rows; disabled while saving or without a value. Options are the intersection for the selected rows, or free text if the plan requires it. This is not equivalent to Apply to selected. | Bulk toolbar, separate per-level controls |
| Rectification result status | Show them / hide them for already complete rows | `setShowHidden` toggles whether completed rows are displayed when `hiddenComplete.size > 0`. Keep distinct from Show all mapped rows. | Result status |
| Rectification custom line editor | Custom caption input; Cancel / Use Custom Line | `setCustomEditor`, `closeCustomEditor`, `submitCustomEditor`; Use Custom Line disabled for blank text; Enter submits, Escape and backdrop cancel; invoked by row or bulk mapping selections. | Custom caption dialog |
| Shell | Keyboard Shortcuts dialog | Ctrl/Cmd + `/` or `?` opens; Radix dialog `onOpenChange` closes; contents include global, tab, FSR and VERA Tools shortcut lists | Shell shortcut dialog |

Denominator input only where row has a denominator; GP numerator becomes read-only when trading-account selections apply (PY also needs a previous TB). | Ratio table and toolbar; retain current label, do not conflate with the separate Tax Audit Form No. 26 |
| Same ratio screen, Gross Profit row | Calculate via Trading A/c / Hide Trading A/c | `setGpExpanded` toggles panel | Gross Profit row |
| Same ratio screen, Trading A/c | Expense and Income group checkboxes and per-item checkboxes | `toggleAllGp(side)` and `toggleGp(side)` persist selection for the respective side; stock values are pulled separately. | Trading Account panel |
| Features > Ratio > Analytical Ratios | Closing balances; Average balances | `setBasis('closing'/'average')`, persisted to localStorage; average basis has different year availability | Analytical Ratios header |
| Features > Ratio > Company Additional Disclosure Ratio | Table has no action controls in `StatutoryRatios`; preserve displayed data and any inherited navigation | Read-only view, conditional on financial data / company | Ratio screen |
| Features > Format > Proprietorship / Partnership / LLP / Company | Download PDF; Download Excel | `savePDF`, `saveExcel`; disabled without generated HTML or while either export is busy; source selects a distinct constitution from the parent navigation. | Format toolbar |
| Check Control | Previous Year TB selector | `PYTBSelector` -> `onPrevTBChange`; absent if there are no eligible alternatives | Check Control header |

## Note-detail form fields: further source review

These are in addition to the form launch and footer actions above. Every
generated row/cell is distinct by its row, period and field key; preserve the
same control and update callback for each instance.

| Old location | Control | Handler / current condition | Proposed location |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Cash and Bank Fill Details | Amount CY, amount PY and remarks for each earmarked, margin/security and repatriation-restriction row | `handleChange(item.key, 'amount' / 'amountPY' / 'remarks', value)`; table hidden during loading; Cancel `onClose`, Save & Close `handleSave` disabled while loading | Cash and Bank dialog |
| Director Loans Fill Details | CY and PY amounts for every director/officer/interested-entity category | `setCell(period, row.key, value)`; amounts non-negative input; displayed only after loading; close icon and Cancel call `onClose`, Save calls `handleSave` and is disabled while loading | Director Loans dialog |
| Trade Receivables, company | CY and PY ageing schedule numeric cells | `setCell(period, row.key, bucket.key, value)` only in applicable cells; computed and blocked cells are not inputs; retain blocked-cell rules | Company ageing tables |
| Trade Receivables, company | Always include in PDF & Excel (even if all zeros); Close / Save | `setSaved(false)` and update `includeInOutput`; `onClose` / `handleSave`; Save disabled while loading | Company ageing dialog |
| Trade Receivables, non-company | Amount by ageing block and secured/unsecured/doubtful category | `setCell(block.key, category.key, value)`; reset to default restores the original distribution; Close / Save as separately recorded above | Non-company ageing table |
| Trade Payables, company | CY/PY ageing schedule inputs and MSME / Others TB break-up in each Check table | `setCell(period, row.key, bucket.key, value)` / `setAgeing`; total and blocked cells are not editable; applicable ageing cells differ by due, undue and unbilled rows | Ageing tab |
| Trade Payables, company | Always include in PDF & Excel (even if all zeros) | Updates `ageing.includeInOutput`; distinct from the MSME output checkbox | Ageing tab |
| Trade Payables, all constitutions | CY/PY principal and interest for item (a); CY/PY amounts for MSME items (b)-(e); Always include MSME details in PDF & Excel | `setA`, `setPlain`, update `msme.includeInOutput`; item (a) total is computed | MSME tab / disclosure |
| Share Capital > Classes | Class name, Equity / Preference type, face value; CY/PY authorised, issued, subscribed, paid-up and reconciliation movements | `setTop`, `set`, `setIssued`; issued auto-fills subscribed and paid-up but both remain editable; calculated amounts are not inputs | Each share-class card |
| Share Capital > Shareholders | Equity and preference shareholder name and shares CY/PY, add/remove row; promoter name, share class, number of shares, % change, add/remove row | `setRow`, `addRow`, `removeRow`, upstream data setters; holding percentages are calculated displays rather than editable inputs | Shareholders and promoters tables |
| Share Capital > Rights / Disclosures / Footnotes | Rights text; each disclosure text and conditional Standard text reset; each footnote text | `setData`, `setDisclosure`; reset appears when disclosure differs from default | Corresponding section |
| Long / Short Term Borrowings > loan card | Repayment Details / Repayment & Terms and Security Details expanders | Native `details` / `summary` controls; separately toggle each loan's sections. They were not captured in the JSX button/input extractor. | Per-loan editor |
| Long / Short Term Borrowings > loan card | Lender print name with suggestions, nature and secured/unsecured; rate, repayment and security format and fields | `LoanEditor.set` -> parent `setLoans`; format buttons choose tabular or paragraph. Both expose security nature, guarantee and charge ranking in tabular mode or security paragraph in paragraph mode. | Per-loan editor |
| Long Term Borrowings > loan card | CY/PY current maturity; repayment rate, instalment amount, frequency, remaining instalments, maturity date, remarks or repayment paragraph | `LoanEditor.set`; gross CY/PY and net values in this card are displayed, not editable controls | Long-term loan editor |
| Short Term Borrowings > loan card | Rate of interest / terms; repayment frequency and remarks or repayment paragraph | `LoanEditor.set`; gross CY/PY amounts in this card are displayed, not editable controls | Short-term loan editor |
| Long Term Borrowings > Print options | Print Repayment Details, Print Security Details, Print Reconciliation | Three distinct setters `setPrintRepayment`, `setPrintSecurity`, `setPrintReconciliation` | Long-term print-options tab |
| Short Term Borrowings > Print options | Print Repayment & Terms, Print Security Details | `setPrintRepayment`, `setPrintSecurity`; no long-term reconciliation checkbox in this form | Short-term print-options tab |
| Owners / Partners Capital, single-table variant | Party name; CY share %, opening, introduced, remuneration, interest, withdrawals and share of profit/loss; aggregate PY opening, introduced, remuneration, interest, withdrawals and profit/loss | `setRow` / `onRowsChange` and `onPyChange`. Share %, remuneration and interest are absent for a single owner; there is no separate PY share-% input. Calculated closings are displays. | Single-account table |
| Owners / Partners Capital, all variants | Share-of-profit override and reset | `ProfitCell` edit/reset appears for the sole proprietor's CY/PY and for partners with a share percentage; a manual override exposes reset. LLP automatic share belongs to the Current Account. Without a percentage a partner has an ordinary input. | Corresponding account rows |
| Owners / Partners Capital, partnership / LLP | Add and remove party / partner | `addRow`, `removeRow`; unavailable for a single owner. | Corresponding account rows |
| Owners / Partners Capital, LLP variant | Partner name and agreed contribution; Contribution and Current Account share %, opening, introduced, remuneration, interest, withdrawals, profit; each table's PY inputs | `setRowTop`, `setRowSub`, `onPyContribChange`, `onPyCurrentChange`; the same partner rows feed both account tables | LLP contribution / current tables |
| Owners / Partners Capital dialog | Close icon, backdrop and Cancel; Save Note Details | `onClose`; `save` disabled while saving; preserve per-TB versus entity-level storage | Form header / footer |

'Loss' : 'Profit'} for the year as per the P&L: <span className="font-mono font-semibold">₹{fmtN(Math.abs(split.profit))}</span></span> <span>Divided by the share %{data.variant === 'llp' ? Clear a box to leave that item out. </p> {SC_DISCLOSURES.map(s => { const isDefault = discTexts[s.key] === defaultDisclosureText(s.key, data) return ( <div key={s.key} className="mb-3"> <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1"> <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700">{noteNum}({s.key}) {s.heading}</label> {!isDefault && ( <button onClick={() => setDisclosure(s.key, null)} className="text-[10px] text-blue-600 hover:underline">↺ Standard text</button> )} </div> <textarea value={discTexts[s.key]} onChange={e => setDisclosure(s.key, e.target.value)} rows={s.key === 'i' ? 5 : 3} className="w-full border border-gray-300 rounded px-2 py-1.5 text-xs text-justify focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-400 resize-y" /> </div> ) })} </div> )} {/* Footnotes */} {activeSection === 'footnotes' && ( <div> <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700 mb-2">Footnotes for Share Capital</div> {data.footnotes.map((fn, i) => ( <div key={i} className="flex items-start gap-2 mb-2"> <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 pt-1 w-5 shrink-0">{i + 1}.</span> <input className={INP + ' flex-1'} value={fn} onChange={e => setData(d => ({ ...d, footnotes: d.footnotes.map((f, fi) => fi === i ? e.target.value : f) }))} placeholder={'Footnote ${i + 1}'} /> </div> ))} </div> )} {/* Footer */} Data is saved per TB and included in generated PDF/Excel. | src/modules/fsg/components/TrialBalance/UploadTB.jsx:114 | Choose Trial Balance Format × Select the source format of your Trial Balance. Simple Excel Format (Trial Balance and Masters) One Excel workbook with two sheets: the Trial Balance (Ledger · Under Group · Opening · Debit · Credit · Closing, in Dr and Cr columns) and the Groups master (Group · Under Group · Nature). Works for any accounting software. Get the blank format with "Download Template". Tally Trial Balance & Chart of Accounts Two files: a Tally TB Excel (Particulars · Opening · Debit · Credit · Closing with Dr/Cr suffixes) and a Tally Master JSON (chart of accounts → builds the Level 1–7 hierarchy automatically). Real Books Format Two files: a Real Books Trial Balance Excel (Ledger Group Details Pivot Structure) and a Real Books Ledger Master Excel. Zoho Books Format One Excel file — Zoho Books' Trial Balance report (Account · Account Code · Opening Balance · Net Debit · Net Credit · Closing Balance). Zoho shows Debit as + and Credit as −; it is converted automatically. Suitable for a branch's Trial Balance too. Busy Accounting Software Format One Excel file — Busy's Trial Balance (Account/Group Name · Opening Bal. · Debit · Credit · Closing Bal., balances with Dr / Cr). The group tree is read from Busy's own indentation and checked against every group total. Suitable for a branch's Trial Balance too. ⚡ Tally Auto-Import (Live) NEW Connect directly to Tally while it is open. TB and Chart of Accounts are imported automatically — no file export needed. Tally must be running on this computer. Cancel | onClick={onCancel} | See source condition | Same screen, pending individual review |
